# SVC metrics — 20 Mbit/s with ±20 ms jitter (2026-11-04)

What was run: the pre-registered SVC metrics protocol on one more link, 20 Mbit/s with ±20 ms of jitter each way, on the three DBT volumes, 13 variants, 1× and 4× CPU.
What it shows: *in progress, 1 of 10 rounds done; the reading comes when the sitting is whole.*
What it rests on: three digital breast tomosynthesis (DBT) volumes only (`dbts_a1`, `dbts_b1`, `dbts_c1`), one 4-core host, headless Chromium 153.0.8010.12; nothing here is claimed for other series, modalities or devices.
What is left: the reading below once all 10 rounds are in; the breast ultrasound cine and ABUS rows, 5 and 50 Mbit/s and 2 % loss, which other sittings run.
The rule's verdict is not applied here; another session applies it once every condition is in.

The protocol is [`docs/av1/README.md`](../../../../../docs/av1/README.md) §The scalable preview as a tool, part 10; how each
number is made is [`README.md`](README.md) §SVC metrics; the first sitting (no limit and 20 Mbit/s, no jitter) is
[`svc-results.md`](svc-results.md), whose method this sitting keeps.

*Rows are pushed after every round; until round 10 the tables are partial.*

## Tables

Visits 78, kept 64, VOID 14; cells 6.
